{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"# Pivot Analysis: Alternative Gate Mechanisms\\n\\n## 当前架构概述\\n\\n### EventGate (`networks.py:336-403`)\\n- 2层 MLP (feat_size → 256 → 256 → K=8 logits)\\n- `forward()`: feat → MLP → logits → mask beyond effective_max_dt → **Gumbel-Softmax(τ)** → hard argmax → `dt_index ∈ {1,...,K}`\\n- τ 从 5.0 线性退火到 0.5（200K steps）\\n- 参数量：feat_size×256 + 256×256 + 256×8 ≈ 200K（feat_size=1536 时）\\n\\n### Imagination 中使用 gate (`models.py:528-591`)\\n```\\nstep_fn: state → feat → action → img_step(state, action, dt=prev_dt) → succ_feat → gate(succ_feat, hard=True) → next_dt\\n```\\n- `prev_dt` 传入 RSSM.img_step，通过 `nn.Embedding(K, 32)` 编码，concat 到 `[stoch, action]` 后输入 GRU\\n- gate 在每一步都 detach feat 后 forward，输出 next_dt 用于下一步\\n\\n### dt-aware λ-return (`models.py:592-637`)\\n- `discount^dt` 替代 `discount`\\n- `lambda_return_variable_dt()` (`tools.py:725-762`) 用 `pcont_adj = pcont^dt` 做递推\\n- weights 也用 `discount^dt` 的累积积\\n\\n### Gate 训练信号 (`models.py:240-278`)\\n- state delta supervision：`compute_gate_targets()` (`models.py:19-43`)\\n  - 大 delta → 小 dt（频繁观察），小 delta → 大 dt（跳步）\\n  - Gaussian kernel → softmax target distribution\\n- `gate_loss_fn()`：logits vs target 的 cross-entropy\\n- gate_scale curriculum：0-25K off，25K-75K 线性 ramp，75K+ full\\n- entropy bonus：从 0.1 退火到 0.01\\n\\n### Replay sub-sampling (`tools.py:763-830`)\\n- 每个 batch element 从 log-uniform[1, effective_max_dt] 采 dt\\n- 按 dt 跳步取帧，累积折扣 reward，取 min continuation\\n\\n---\\n\\n## 方案 A: Soft Attention Mixture\\n\\n### 核心思路\\ngate 输出 logits → softmax weights（不用 Gumbel-Softmax/argmax）→ 对 K 个候选 dt 的 latent transition 做加权平均。\\n\\n### 改动文件/函数\\n\\n| 文件 | 函数/位置 | 改动内容 | 行数 |\\n|------|-----------|----------|------|\\n| `networks.py:377-398` | `EventGate.forward()` | ��掉 Gumbel-Softmax，改用 softmax 返回 soft weights；去掉 hard 参数 | ~5 |\\n| `networks.py:241-279` | `RSSM.img_step()` | 新增 `img_step_mixture()` 方法：对 K 个 dt 分别做 forward，用 soft weights 加权平均 stoch/deter | ~35 |\\n| `models.py:548-564` | `_imagine()` step_fn | `succ = dynamics.img_step_mixture(state, action, weights)` 替代单 dt 调用 | ~15 |\\n| `models.py:592-637` | `_compute_target()` | dt 改为 expected_dt = Σ(w_k × k)（float），传入 lambda_return_variable_dt | ~5 |\\n| `models.py:494-522` | gate metrics | dt_mean/std 用 expected_dt（float）计算 | ~5 |\\n\\n### 预估代码行数\\n~60-80 行改动，3 个文件。\\n\\n### 与当前训练 pipeline 兼容性\\n- WorldModel._train 的 gate loss 不需要改——仍用 logits 做 cross-entropy supervision\\n- subsample_variable_dt 不需要改（训练时 dt 仍是整数采样）\\n- lambda_return_variable_dt 的 dt 参数已支持 float，无需修改\\n\\n### 风险点\\n1. **计算量 ×K**：每个 imagination step 需要 K=8 次 RSSM forward，memory 和 FLOPs ×8。以当前 horizon=15 计算，imagination 从 15 次 img_step 变成 15×8=120 次\\n2. **模糊状态问题**：对 K 个离散 RSSM state 做加权平均，可能产生不在 learned manifold 上的\\\"模糊\\\"中间状态，导致 decoder/reward head 预测不稳定\\n3. **可选缓解**：先加权平均 dt_embedding 再做一次 forward（计算量不变），但表达能力弱——相当于 soft dt embedding 而非 mixture of transitions\\n\\n---\\n\\n## 方案 B: Binary Skip (dt ∈ {1, K})\\n\\n### 核心思路\\ngate 从 K 类分类退化为二分类：skip（dt=K）或 no-skip（dt=1）。\\n\\n### 改动文件/函数\\n\\n| 文件 | 函数/位置 | 改动内容 | 行数 |\\n|------|-----------|----------|------|\\n| `networks.py:369` | `EventGate.__init__` logit_layer | `nn.Linear(hidden_units, 2)` 替代 `nn.Linear(hidden_units, max_dt)` | ~2 |\\n| `networks.py:377-398` | `EventGate.forward()` | logits 2-dim；`dt_index = argmax → map {0→1, 1→K}` | ~5 |\\n| `networks.py:400-403` | `EventGate.entropy()` | 自动适配（2-dim logits），无需改 | 0 |\\n| `models.py:19-43` | `compute_gate_targets()` | 简化为 2-dim target：delta_norm > threshold → [1,0] (dt=1)，否则 [0,1] (dt=K) | ~10 |\\n| `models.py:246-254` | WorldModel._train gate logits | `gate_max_dt` 相关 reshape 改为 2 | ~3 |\\n| `tools.py:763-830` | `subsample_variable_dt()` | dt 只采 1 或 K（Bernoulli 替代 log-uniform） | ~5 |\\n| `models.py:494-522` | gate metrics | bincount 范围改为 2；dt_frac 只有 2 个 | ~3 |\\n\\n### 预估代码行数\\n~25-30 行改动，3 个文件。\\n\\n### 与当前训练 pipeline 兼容性\\n- RSSM dt_embedding 仍工作（只用 index 0 和 K-1），可选减小 Embedding(K,32) → Embedding(2,32) 但非必须\\n- _imagine 中 img_step(dt=prev_dt) 不需要改——dt 仍是整数\\n- _compute_target 和 lambda_return_variable_dt 不需要改——dt 仍是整数\\n- 检查点不兼容（logit_layer 权重维度从 K 变 2）��需从头训练\\n\\n### 风险点\\n1. **跳步粒度丧失**：只能 dt=1 或 dt=8，中间值没了。如果最优策略需要 dt=3-4，binary 无法表达\\n2. **K=8 跳步过大**：二分类下 skip 直接跳 8 步，可能太激进。可配合降低 K 到 4 或 2\\n3. **收敛快**：2 类分类比 8 类简单得多，gate 应该更快收敛，适合快速验证 skip 概念是否有价值\\n\\n---\\n\\n## 方案 C: Fixed K=2 (No Learned Gate)\\n\\n### 核心思路\\n去掉 gate MLP 和 Gumbel-Softmax，固定每步 dt=2。纯 ablation baseline，用于判断 variable-rate 本身有没有价值。\\n\\n### 改动文件/函数\\n\\n| 文件 | 函数/位置 | 改动内容 | 行数 |\\n|------|-----------|----------|------|\\n| `configs.yaml:101-113` | gate config | 新增 `gate_mode: fixed`，`gate_fixed_dt: 2` | ~2 |\\n| `models.py:528-591` | `_imagine()` | gate_enabled 分支中：替换 gate 调用为 `next_dt = fixed_dt`（常数 tensor） | ~5 |\\n| `models.py:170-290` | `WorldModel._train()` | gate_mode=fixed 时跳过 gate loss 计算，但保留 subsample_variable_dt（用 fixed dt） | ~8 |\\n| `tools.py:763-830` | `subsample_variable_dt()` | 支持 fixed_dt 参数：所有 batch element 用相同 dt | ~5 |\\n| `models.py:494-522` | gate metrics | gate_mode=fixed 时不记 gate 分布指标，只记 dt=2 | ~3 |\\n\\n### 预估代码行数\\n~20 行改动，3 个文件 + config。\\n\\n### 与当前训练 pipeline 兼容性\\n- dt=2 的 dt_embedding 仍工作（用 index 1）\\n- lambda_return_variable_dt 直接传 dt=2 的常数 tensor\\n- gate 参数不加入 optimizer（或直接 gate_enabled=false + 额外逻辑）\\n- 可复用现有 gate_enabled=false 分支，只需在 non-gate _imagine 中加 dt=2 支持\\n\\n### 风险点\\n1. **风险极低**：最简单的改动，几乎不可能引入 bug\\n2. **目的明确**：如果 fixed dt=2 表现接近或优于 learned gate，说明 gate 机制本身有问题（而非 variable-rate 概念）\\n3. **如果 fixed dt=2 也掉 return**，说明跳步本身就有害，应该���退到 vanilla DreamerV3 (dt=1)\\n\\n---\\n\\n## 对比总结\\n\\n| 维度 | 方案 A: Soft Mixture | 方案 B: Binary Skip | 方案 C: Fixed dt=2 |\\n|------|---------------------|--------------------|--------------------|\\n| **改动量** | ~60-80 行 / 3 文件 | ~25-30 行 / 3 文件 | ~20 行 / 3 文件 + config |\\n| **计算开销** | ×K（imagination ×8） | 同当前 | 同当前 |\\n| **梯度信号** | 最丰富（连续 weights） | 二分类，信号清晰 | 无 gate 梯度 |\\n| **表达能力** | 最强（K 个 dt 的混合） | 最弱（只有 1 和 K） | 无（固定） |\\n| **实现风险** | 中（模糊状态问题） | 低 | 极低 |\\n| **诊断价值** | 低（改太多，难定位原因） | 中（验证 skip 概念） | 高（验证 dt>1 是否有害） |\\n| **检查点兼容** | 否 | 否 | 是（可复用 gate_enabled=false 路径） |\\n| **推荐优先级** | 3（最后尝试） | 2 | 风险点\\\\n1. **计算量 ×K**：每个 imagination step 需要 K=8 次 RSSM forward，memory 和 FLOPs ×8。以当前 horizon=15 计算，imagination 从 15 次 img_step 变成 15×8=120 次\\\\n2. **模糊状态问题**：对 K 个离散 RSSM state 做加权平均，可能产生不在 learned manifold 上的\\\\\\\"模糊\\\\\\\"中间状态，导致 decoder/reward head 预测不稳定\\\\n3. **可选缓解**：先加权平均 dt_embedding 再做一次 forward（计算量不变），但表达能力弱——相当于 soft dt embedding 而非 mixture of transitions\\\\n\\\\n---\\\\n\\\\n## 方案 B: Binary Skip (dt ∈ {1, K})\\\\n\\\\n### 核心思路\\\\ngate 从 K 类分类退化为二分类：skip（dt=K）或 no-skip（dt=1）。\\\\n\\\\n### 改动文件/函数\\\\n\\\\n| 文件 | 函数/位置 | 改动内容 | 行数 |\\\\n|------|-----------|----------|------|\\\\n| `networks.py:369` | `EventGate.__init__` logit_layer | `nn.Linear(hidden_units, 2)` 替代 `nn.Linear(hidden_units, max_dt)` | ~2 |\\\\n| img_step(dt=prev_dt) 不需要改——dt 仍是整数\\\\n- _compute_target 和 lambda_return_variable_dt 不需要改——dt 仍是整数\\\\n- 检查点不兼容（logit_layer 权重维度从 K 变 2）��需从头训练\\\\n\\\\n### 风险点\\\\n1. **跳步粒度丧失**：只能 dt=1 或 K=2 (No Learned Gate)\\\\n\\\\n### 核心思路\\\\n去掉 gate MLP 和 Gumbel-Softmax，固定每步 dt=2。纯 ablation baseline，用于判断 variable-rate 本身有没有价值。\\\\n\\\\n### 改动文件/函数\\\\n\\\\n| 文件 | 函数/位置 | 改动内容 | 行数 |\\\\n|------|-----------|----------|------|\\\\n| `configs.yaml:101-113` | gate config | 新增 `gate_mode: fixed`，`gate_fixed_dt: 2` | ~2 |\\\\n| `models.py:528-591` | `_imagine()` | gate_enabled 分支中：替换 gate 调用为 `next_dt = fixed_dt`（常数 tensor） | ~5 |\\\\n| `models.py:170-290` | `WorldModel._train()` | gate_mode=fixed 时跳过 gate loss 计算，但保留 subsample_variable_dt（用 fixed dt） | ~8 |\\\\n| `tools.py:763-830` | `subsample_variable_dt()` | 支持 fixed_dt 参数：所有 batch element 用相同 dt | ~5 |\\\\n| `models.py:494-522` | gate metrics | gate_mode=fixed 时不记 gate 分布指标，只记 dt=2 | ~3 |\\\\n\\\\n### 预估代码行数\\\\n~20 行改动，3 个文件 + config。\\\\n\\\\n### 与当前训练 pipeline 兼容性\\\\n- dt=2 的 dt_embedding 仍工作（用 index 1）\\\\n- lambda_return_variable_dt 直接传 dt=2 的常数 tensor\\\\n- gate 参数不加入 optimizer（或直接 gate_enabled=false + 额外逻辑）\\\\n- 可复用现有 gate_enabled=false 分支，只需在 non-gate _imagine 中加 dt=2 支持\\\\n\\\\n### 风险点\\\\n1. **风险极低**：最简单的改动，几乎不可能引入 bug\\\\n2. **目的明确**：如果 fixed dt=2 表现接近或优于 learned gate，说明 ×K（imagination ×8） | 同当前 | 同当前 |\\\\n| **梯度信号** | 最丰富（连续 weights） | 二分类，信号清晰 | 无 gate 梯度 |\\\\n| **表达能力** | 最强（K 个 dt 的混合） | 最弱（只有 1 和 K） | 无（固定） |\\\\n| **实现风险** | 中（模糊状态问题） | 低 | 极低 |\\\\n| **诊断价值** feats[:-1], dim=-1)  # (time-1, batch)\\n    delta_min = deltas.min(dim=0, keepdim=True).values\\n    delta_max = deltas.max(dim=0, keepdim=True).values\\n    delta_norm = (deltas - delta_min) / (delta_max - delta_min + 1e-8)\\n    preferred_dt = 1.0 + (eff - 1.0) * (1.0 - delta_norm)\\n    dt_indices = torch.arange(1, max_dt + 1, dtype=torch.float32, device=feats.device)\\n    sigma = 1.0\\n    log_probs = -0.5 * ((preferred_dt.unsqueeze(-1) - dt_indices) / sigma) ** 2\\n    if eff < max_dt:\\n        log_probs[..., eff:] = -1e9\\n    target_dist = F.softmax(log_probs, dim=-1)\\n    return target_dist\\n\\n\\ndef gate_loss_fn(gate_logits, target_dist):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Cross-entropy between gate predictions and supervision target.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    log_probs = F.log_softmax(gate_logits, dim=-1)\\n    return -(target_dist * log_probs).sum(dim=-1)\\n\\n\\nclass RewardEMA:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"running mean and std\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n    def __init__(self, device, alpha=1e-2):\\n        self.device = device\\n        self.alpha = alpha\\n        self.range = torch.tensor([0.05, 0.95], device=device)\\n\\n    def __call__(self, x, ema_vals):\\n        flat_x = torch.flatten(x.detach())\\n        x_quantile = torch.quantile(input=flat_x, q=self.range)\\n        # this should be in-place operation\\n        ema_vals[:] = self.alpha * x_quantile + (1 - self.alpha) * ema_vals\\n        scale = torch.clip(ema_vals[1] - ema_vals[0], min=1.0)\\n        offset = ema_vals[0]\\n        return offset.detach(), scale.detach()\\n\\n\\nclass WorldModel(nn.Module):\\n    def __init__(self, obs_space, act_space, step, config):\\n        super(WorldModel, self).__init__()\\n        self._step = step\\n        self._use_amp = True if config.precision == 16 else False\\n        self._config = config\\n        shapes = {k: tuple(v.shape) for k, v in obs_space.spaces.items()}\\n        self.encoder = networks.MultiEncoder(shapes, **config.encoder)\\n        self.embed_size = self.encoder.outdim\\n        self.dynamics = networks.RSSM(\\n            config.dyn_stoch,\\n            config.dyn_deter,\\n            config.dyn_hidden,\\n            config.dyn_rec_depth,\\n            config.dyn_discrete,\\n            config.act,\\n            config.norm,\\n            config.dyn_mean_act,\\n            config.dyn_std_act,\\n            config.dyn_min_std,\\n            config.unimix_ratio,\\n            config.initial,\\n            config.num_actions,\\n            self.embed_size,\\n            config.device,\\n            max_dt=config.gate_max_dt if config.gate_enabled else 1,\\n            dt_embed_dim=config.gate_dt_embed_dim,\\n        )\\n        self.heads = nn.ModuleDict()\\n        if config.dyn_discrete:\\n            feat_size = config.dyn_stoch * config.dyn_discrete + config.dyn_deter\\n        else:\\n            feat_size = config.dyn_stoch + config.dyn_deter\\n        self.heads[\\\"decoder\\\"] = networks.MultiDecoder(\\n            feat_size, shapes, **config.decoder\\n        )\\n        self.heads[\\\"reward\\\"] = networks.MLP(\\n            feat_size,\\n            (255,) if config.reward_head[\\\"dist\\\"] == \\\"symlog_disc\\\" else (),\\n            config.reward_head[\\\"layers\\\"],\\n            config.units,\\n            config.act,\\n    \\n\\n... succ.items()\\n            }\\n            return feats, states, actions\\n\\n    def _compute_target(self, imag_feat, imag_state, reward, dts=None):\\n        if \\\"cont\\\" in self._world_model.heads:\\n            inp = self._world_model.dynamics.get_feat(imag_state)\\n            discount = self._config.discount * self._world_model.heads[\\\"cont\\\"](inp).mean\\n        else:\\n            discount = self._config.discount * torch.ones_like(reward)\\n        value = self.value(imag_feat).mode()\\n\\n        if dts is not None and self._config.gate_enabled:\\n            H = reward.shape[0]\\n            init_dt_tensor = torch.ones_like(dts[:1])\\n            transition_dts = torch.cat([init_dt_tensor, dts[:H-2]], 0)\\n\\n            target = tools.lambda_return_variable_dt(\\n                reward[1:],\\n                value[:-1],\\n                discount[1:],\\n                transition_dts,\\n                bootstrap=value[-1],\\n                lambda_=self._config.discount_lambda,\\n                axis=0,\\n            )\\n            transition_dts_full = torch.cat([init_dt_tensor, dts[:-1]], 0)\\n            pcont_adj = discount ** transition_dts_full.unsqueeze(-1).float()\\n            weights = torch.cumprod(\\n                torch.cat([torch.ones_like(pcont_adj[:1]), pcont_adj[:-1]], 0), 0\\n            ).detach()\\n        else:\\n            target = tools.lambda_return(\\n                reward[1:],\\n                value[:-1],\\n                discount[1:],\\n                bootstrap=value[-1],\\n                lambda_=self._config.discount_lambda,\\n                axis=0,\\n            )\\n            weights = torch.cumprod(\\n                torch.cat([torch.ones_like(discount[:1]), discount[:-1]], 0), 0\\n            ).detach()\\n        return target, weights, value[:-1]\\n\\n    def _compute_actor_loss(\\n        self,\\n        imag_feat,\\n        imag_action,\\n        target,\\n        weights,\\n        base,\\n    ):\\n        metrics = {}\\n        inp = imag_feat.detach()\\n        policy = self.actor(inp)\\n        # Q-val for actor is not transformed using symlog\\n        target = torch.stack(target, dim=1)\\n        if self._config.reward_EMA:\\n            offset, scale = self.reward_ema(target, self.ema_vals)\\n            normed_target = (target - offset) / scale\\n            normed_base = (base - offset) / scale\\n            adv = normed_target - normed_base\\n            metrics.update(tools.tensorstats(normed_target, \\\"normed_target\\\"))\\n            metrics[\\\"EMA_005\\\"] = to_np(self.ema_vals[0])\\n            metrics[\\\"EMA_095\\\"] = to_np(self.ema_vals[1])\\n        else:\\n            adv = target - base\\n\\n        if self._config.imag_gradient == \\\"dynamics\\\":\\n            actor_target = adv\\n        elif self._config.imag_gradient == \\\"reinforce\\\":\\n            actor_target = (\\n                policy.log_prob(imag_action)[:-1][:, :, None]\\n                * (target - self.value(imag_feat[:-1]).mode()).detach()\\n            )\\n        elif self._config.imag_gradient == \\\"both\\\":\\n            actor_target = (\\n                policy.log_prob(imag_action)[:-1][:, :, None]\\n                * (target - self.value(imag_feat[:-1]).mode()).detach()\\n            )\\n            mix = self._config.imag_gradient_mix\\n            actor_target = mix * target + (1 - mix) * actor_target\\n            metrics[\\\"imag_gradient_mix\\\"] = mix\\n        else:\\n            raise NotImplementedError(self._config.imag_gradient)\\n        actor_loss = -weights[:-1] * actor_target\\n        return actor_loss, metrics\\n\\n    def _update_slow_target(self):\\n        if self._config.critic[\\\"slow_target\\\"]:\\n            if self._updates % self._config.critic[\\\"slow_target_update\\\"] == 0:\\n                mix = self._config.critic[\\\"slow_target_fraction\\\"]\\n                for s, d in zip(self.value.parameters(), self._slow_value.parameters()):\\n                    d.data = mix * s.data + (1 - mix) * d.data\\n            self._updates += 1\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": def random_agent(o, d, s):\\n            action = random_actor.sample()\\n            logprob = random_actor.log_prob(action)\\n            return {\\\"action\\\": action, \\\"logprob\\\": logprob}, None\\n\\n        state = tools.simulate(\\n            random_agent,\\n            train_envs,\\n            train_eps,\\n            config.traindir,\\n            logger,\\n            limit=config.dataset_size,\\n            steps=prefill,\\n        )\\n        logger.step += prefill * config.action_repeat\\n        print(f\\\"Logger: ({logger.step} steps).\\\")\\n\\n    print(\\\"Simulate agent.\\\")\\n    train_dataset = make_dataset(train_eps, config)\\n    eval_dataset = make_dataset(eval_eps, config)\\n    agent = Dreamer(\\n        train_envs[0].observation_space,\\n        train_envs[0].action_space,\\n        config,\\n        logger,\\n        train_dataset,\\n    ).to(config.device)\\n    agent.requires_grad_(requires_grad=False)\\n    if (logdir / \\\"latest.pt\\\").exists():\\n        checkpoint = torch.load(logdir / \\\"latest.pt\\\")\\n        agent.load_state_dict(checkpoint[\\\"agent_state_dict\\\"])\\n        tools.recursively_load_optim_state_dict(agent, checkpoint[\\\"optims_state_dict\\\"])\\n        agent._should_pretrain._once = False\\n\\n    # make sure eval will be executed once after config.steps\\n    while agent._step < config.steps + config.eval_every:\\n        logger.write()\\n        if config.eval_episode_num > 0:\\n            print(\\\"Start evaluation.\\\")\\n            eval_policy = functools.partial(agent, training=False)\\n            tools.simulate(\\n                eval_policy,\\n                eval_envs,\\n                eval_eps,\\n                config.evaldir,\\n                logger,\\n                is_eval=True,\\n                episodes=config.eval_episode_num,\\n            )\\n            if config.video_pred_log:\\n                video_pred = agent._wm.video_pred(next(eval_dataset))\\n                logger.video(\\\"eval_openl\\\", to_np(video_pred))\\n        print(\\\"Start training.\\\")\\n        state = tools.simulate(\\n            agent,\\n            train_envs,\\n            train_eps,\\n            config.traindir,\\n            logger,\\n            limit=config.dataset_size,\\n            steps=config.eval_every,\\n            state=state,\\n        )\\n        items_to_save = {\\n            \\\"agent_state_dict\\\": agent.state_dict(),\\n            \\\"optims_state_dict\\\": tools.recursively_collect_optim_state_dict(agent),\\n        }\\n        torch.save(items_to_save, logdir / \\\"latest.pt\\\")\\n    for env in train_envs + eval_envs:\\n        try:\\n            env.close()\\n        except Exception:\\n            pass\\n\\n\\nif __name__ == \\\"__main__\\\":\\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--configs\\\", nargs=\\\"+\\\")\\n    args, remaining = parser.parse_known_args()\\n    configs = yaml.safe_load(\\n        (pathlib.Path(sys.argv[0]).parent / \\\"configs.yaml\\\").read_text()\\n    )\\n\\n    def recursive_update(base, update):\\n        for key, value in update.items():\\n            if isinstance(value, dict) and key in base:\\n                recursive_update(base[key], value)\\n            else:\\n                base[key] = value\\n\\n    name_list = [\\\"defaults\\\", *args.configs] if args.configs else [\\\"defaults\\\"]\\n    defaults = {}\\n    for name in name_list:\\n        recursive_update(defaults, configs[name])\\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()\\n    for key, value in sorted(defaults.items(), key=lambda x: x[0]):\\n        arg_type = tools.args_type(value)\\n        parser.add_argument(f\\\"--{key}\\\", type=arg_type, default=arg_type(value))\\n    main(parser.parse_args(remaining))\\n\", \"stderr\": {"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"# Event-Gated Adaptive-Horizon DreamerV3 — World Model & Behavior\\n# Modified from NM512/dreamerv3-torch.\\n# Changes: WorldModel integrates EventGate + variable-dt sub-sampling;\\n#          ImagBehavior uses gate-driven dt in imagination + adapted lambda-returns.\\n\\nimport copy\\nimport math\\n\\nimport torch\\nfrom torch import nn\\nfrom torch.nn import functional as F\\n\\nimport networks\\nimport tools\\n\\nto_np = lambda x: x.detach().cpu().numpy()\\n\\n\\ndef compute_gate_targets(feats, max_dt, effective_max_dt=None):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Soft supervision target for the event gate derived from state deltas.\\n\\n    Large delta -> prefer small dt (index 0 = dt=1).\\n    Small delta -> prefer large dt (index K-1 = dt=K).\\n    Probability mass is placed only within [1, effective_max_dt].\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    if effective_max_dt is None:\\n        effective_max_dt = max_dt\\n    eff = max(1, int(effective_max_dt))\\n    feats = feats.detach()\\n    deltas = torch.norm(feats[1:] - feats[:-1], dim=-1)  # (time-1, batch)\\n    delta_min = deltas.min(dim=0, keepdim=True).values\\n    delta_max = deltas.max(dim=0, keepdim=True).values\\n    delta_norm = (deltas - delta_min) / (delta_max - delta_min + 1e-8)\\n    preferred_dt = 1.0 + (eff - 1.0) * (1.0 - delta_norm)\\n    dt_indices = torch.arange(1, max_dt + 1, dtype=torch.float32, device=feats.device)\\n    sigma = 1.0\\n    log_probs = -0.5 * ((preferred_dt.unsqueeze(-1) - dt_indices) / sigma) ** 2\\n    if eff < max_dt:\\n        log_probs[..., eff:] = -1e9\\n    target_dist = F.softmax(log_probs, dim=-1)\\n    return target_dist\\n\\n\\ndef gate_loss_fn(gate_logits, target_dist):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Cross-entropy between gate predictions and supervision target.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    log_probs = F.log_softmax(gate_logits, dim=-1)\\n    return -(target_dist * log_probs).sum(dim=-1)\\n\\n\\nclass RewardEMA:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"running mean and std\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n    def __init__(self, device, alpha=1e-2):\\n        self.device = device\\n        self.alpha = alpha\\n        self.range = torch.tensor([0.05, 0.95], device=device)\\n\\n    def __call__(self, x, ema_vals):\\n        flat_x = torch.flatten(x.detach())\\n        x_quantile = torch.quantile(input=flat_x, q=self.range)\\n        # this should be in-place operation\\n        ema_vals[:] = self.alpha * x_quantile + (1 - self.alpha) * ema_vals\\n        scale = torch.clip(ema_vals[1] - ema_vals[0], min=1.0)\\n        offset = ema_vals[0]\\n        return offset.detach(), scale.detach()\\n\\n\\nclass WorldModel(nn.Module):\\n    def __init__(self, obs_space, act_space, step, config):\\n        super(WorldModel, self).__init__()\\n        self._step = step\\n        self._use_amp = True if config.precision == 16 else False\\n        self._config = config\\n        shapes = {k: tuple(v.shape) for k, v in obs_space.spaces.items()}\\n        self.encoder = networks.MultiEncoder(shapes, **config.encoder)\\n        self.embed_size = self.encoder.outdim\\n        self.dynamics = networks.RSSM(\\n            config.dyn_stoch,\\n            config.dyn_deter,\\n            config.dyn_hidden,\\n            config.dyn_rec_depth,\\n            config.dyn_discrete,\\n            config.act,\\n            config.norm,\\n            config.dyn_mean_act,\\n            config.dyn_std_act,\\n            config.dyn_min_std,\\n            config.unimix_ratio,\\n            config.initial,\\n            config.num_actions,\\n            self.embed_size,\\n            config.device,\\n            max_dt=config.gate_max_dt if config.gate_enabled else 1,\\n            dt_embed_dim=config.gate_dt_embed_dim,\\n        )\\n        self.heads = nn.ModuleDict()\\n        if config.dyn_discrete:\\n            feat_size = config.dyn_stoch * config.dyn_discrete + config.dyn_deter\\n        else:\\n            feat_size = config.dyn_stoch + config.dyn_deter\\n        self.heads[\\\"decoder\\\"] = networks.MultiDecoder(\\n            feat_size, shapes, **config.decoder\\n        )\\n        self.heads[\\\"reward\\\"] = networks.MLP(\\n            feat_size,\\n            (255,) if config.reward_head[\\\"dist\\\"] == \\\"symlog_disc\\\" else (),\\n            config.reward_head[\\\"layers\\\"],\\n            config.units,\\n            config.act,\\n    \\n\\n... [截断: 输出过长，省略 5327 字符] ...\\n\\ntep, hard=False, effective_max_dt=effective_max_dt\\n                    )\\n                    gate_logits = gate_logits_flat.reshape(\\n                        B_g, T_g, self._config.gate_max_dt\\n                    )\\n\\n                    feat_time = gate_feat.permute(1, 0, 2)\\n                    target_dist = compute_gate_targets(\\n                        feat_time, self._config.gate_max_dt,\\n                        effective_max_dt=effective_max_dt,\\n                    )\\n\\n                    g_logits = gate_logits[:, 1:, :].permute(1, 0, 2)\\n                    g_loss = gate_loss_fn(g_logits, target_dist)\\n\\n                    g_entropy = self.event_gate.entropy(\\n                        g_logits.reshape(-1, self._config.gate_max_dt)\\n                    ).reshape(g_loss.shape)\\n\\n                    entropy_frac = min(\\n                        step / max(self._config.gate_tau_anneal_steps, 1), 1.0\\n                    )\\n                    effective_entropy_bonus = (\\n                        self._config.gate_entropy_bonus_init\\n                        + entropy_frac\\n                        * (\\n                            self._config.gate_entropy_bonus_final\\n                            - self._config.gate_entropy_bonus_init\\n                        )\\n                    )\\n\\n                    total_gate_loss = gate_scale * (\\n                        self._config.gate_loss_weight * g_loss.mean()\\n                        - effective_entropy_bonus * g_entropy.mean()\\n                    )\\n                    mean_model_loss = mean_model_loss + total_gate_loss\\n\\n            metrics = self._model_opt(mean_model_loss, self.parameters())\\n\\n        # (batch_size, batch_length) -> (batch_size, batch_length, 1)\\n            obs[\\\"discount\\\"] = obs[\\\"discount\\\"].unsqueeze(-1)\\n        # 'is_first' is necesarry to initialize hidden state at training\\n        assert \\\"is_first\\\" in obs\\n        # 'is_terminal' is necesarry to train cont_head\\n        assert \\\"is_terminal\\\" in obs\\n        obs[\\\"cont\\\"] = (1.0 - obs[\\\"is_terminal\\\"]).unsqueeze(-1)\\n        return obs\\n\\n    def video_pred(self, data):\\n        data = self.preprocess(data)\\n        embed = self.encoder(data)\\n\\n        states, _ = self.dynamics.observe(\\n            embed[:6, :5], data[\\\"action\\\"][:6, :5], data[\\\"is_first\\\"][:6, :5]\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.39}}"}

EventGate + variable-dt sub-sampling;\\n#          ImagBehavior uses gate-driven dt in imagination + adapted lambda-returns.\\n\\nimport copy\\nimport math\\n\\nimport torch\\nfrom torch import nn\\nfrom torch.nn import functional as F\\n\\nimport networks\\nimport tools\\n\\nto_np = lambda x: x.detach().cpu().numpy()\\n\\n\\ndef compute_gate_targets(feats, max_dt, effective_max_dt=None):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Soft supervision target for the event gate derived from state deltas.\\n\\n    Large delta -> prefer small dt (index 0 = dt=1).\\n    Small delta -> prefer large dt (index K-1 = dt=K).\\n    Probability mass is placed only within [1, effective_max_dt].\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    if effective_max_dt is None:\\n        effective_max_dt = max_dt\\n    eff = max(1, int(effective_max_dt))\\n    feats = feats.detach()\\n    deltas = torch.norm(feats[1:] - feats[:-1], dim=-1)  # (time-1, batch)\\n    delta_min = deltas.min(dim=0, keepdim=True).values\\n    delta_max = deltas.max(dim=0, keepdim=True).values\\n    delta_norm = (deltas - delta_min) / (delta_max - delta_min + 1e-8)\\n    preferred_dt = 1.0 + (eff - 1.0) * (1.0 - delta_norm)\\n    dt_indices = torch.arange(1, max_dt + 1, dtype=torch.float32, device=feats.device)\\n    sigma = 1.0\\n    log_probs = -0.5 * ((preferred_dt.unsqueeze(-1) - dt_indices) / sigma) ** 2\\n    if eff < max_dt:\\n        log_probs[..., eff:] = -1e9\\n    target_dist = F.softmax(log_probs, dim=-1)\\n    return target_dist\\n\\n\\ndef gate_loss_fn(gate_logits, target_dist):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Cross-entropy between gate predictions and supervision target.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    log_probs = F.log_softmax(gate_logits, dim=-1)\\n    return -(target_dist * log_probs).sum(dim=-1)\\n\\n\\nclass RewardEMA:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"running mean and std\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n    def __init__(self, device, alpha=1e-2):\\n        self.device = device\\n        self.alpha = alpha\\n        self.range = torch.tensor([0.05, 0.95], device=device)\\n\\n    def __call__(self, x, ema_vals):\\n        flat_x = torch.flatten(x.detach())\\n        x_quantile = torch.quantile(input=flat_x, q=self.range)\\n        # this should be in-place operation\\n        ema_vals[:] = self.alpha * x_quantile + (1 - self.alpha) * ema_vals\\n        scale = torch.clip(ema_vals[1] - ema_vals[0], min=1.0)\\n        offset = ema_vals[0]\\n        return offset.detach(), scale.detach()\\n\\n\\nclass WorldModel(nn.Module):\\n    def __init__(self, obs_space, act_space, step, config):\\n        super(WorldModel, self).__init__()\\n        self._step = step\\n        self._use_amp = True if config.precision == 16 else False\\n        self._config = config\\n        shapes = {k: tuple(v.shape) for k, v in obs_space.spaces.items()}\\n        self.encoder = networks.MultiEncoder(shapes, **config.encoder)\\n        self.embed_size = self.encoder.outdim\\n        self.dynamics = networks.RSSM(\\n            config.dyn_stoch,\\n            config.dyn_deter,\\n            config.dyn_hidden,\\n            config.dyn_rec_depth,\\n            config.dyn_discrete,\\n            config.act,\\n            config.norm,\\n            config.dyn_mean_act,\\n            config.dyn_std_act,\\n            config.dyn_min_std,\\n            config.unimix_ratio,\\n            config.initial,\\n            config.num_actions,\\n            self.embed_size,\\n            config.device,\\n            max_dt=config.gate_max_dt if config.gate_enabled else 1,\\n            dt_embed_dim=config.gate_dt_embed_dim,\\n        )\\n        self.heads = nn.ModuleDict()\\n        if config.dyn_discrete:\\n            feat_size = config.dyn_stoch * config.dyn_discrete + config.dyn_deter\\n        else:\\n            feat_size = config.dyn_stoch + config.dyn_deter\\n        self.heads[\\\"decoder\\\"] = networks.MultiDecoder(\\n            feat_size, shapes, **config.decoder\\n        )\\n        self.heads[\\\"reward\\\"] = networks.MLP(\\n            feat_size,\\n            (255,) if config.reward_head[\\\"dist\\\"] == \\\"symlog_disc\\\" else (),\\n            config.reward_head[\\\"layers\\\"],\\n            config.units,\\n            config.act,\\n            config.norm,\\n            dist=config.reward_head[\\\"dist\\\"],\\n            outscale=config.reward_head[\\\"outscale\\\"],\\n            device=config.device,\\n            name=\\\"Reward\\\",\\n        )\\n        self.heads[\\\"cont\\\"] = networks.MLP(\\n            feat_size,\\n            (),\\n            config.cont_head[\\\"layers\\\"],\\n            config.units,\\n            config.act,\\n            config.norm,\\n            dist=\\\"binary\\\",\\n            outscale=config.cont_head[\\\"outscale\\\"],\\n            device=config.device,\\n            name=\\\"Cont\\\",\\n        )\\n\\n        if config.gate_enabled:\\n            self.event_gate = hidden_layers=config.gate_hidden_layers,\\n                act=config.act,\\n                norm=config.norm,\\n                tau_init=config.gate_tau_init,\\n                tau_final=config.gate_tau_final,\\n                tau_anneal_steps=config.gate_tau_anneal_steps,\\n            )\\n            self.register_buffer(\\n                \\\"gate_step\\\", config.grad_heads:\\n            assert name in self.heads, name\\n        self._model_opt = tools.Optimizer(\\n            \\\"model\\\",\\n            self.parameters(),\\n            config.model_lr,\\n            config.opt_eps,\\n            config.grad_clip,\\n            config.weight_decay,\\n            opt=config.opt,\\n            use_amp=self._use_amp,\\n        )\\n self._step\\n        if step < 25000:\\n            self._gate_scale = 0.0\\n        elif step < 75000:\\n            self._gate_scale = (step - 25000) / (75000 - 25000)\\n        else:\\n            self._gate_scale = 1.0\\n        self._effective_max_dt = 1 + self._gate_scale * (self._config.gate_max_dt - 1)\\n\\n    def _train(self, data):\\n        # action (batch_size, batch_length, act_dim)\\n        # image (batch_size, batch_length, h, w, ch)\\n        # reward (batch_size, batch_length)\\n        # discount (batch_size, batch_length)\\n        data = self.preprocess(data)\\n\\n        if self._config.gate_enabled:\\n            self.gate_step += 1\\n            self._update_gate_schedule()\\n            effective_max_dt = self._effective_max_dt\\n            gate_scale = self._gate_scale\\n            data, dt_labels = tools.subsample_variable_dt(\\n                data, self._config.gate_max_dt, self._config.discount,\\n                effective_max_dt=effective_max_dt,\\n            )\\n        else:\\n            dt_labels = None\\n\\n        with tools.RequiresGrad(self):\\n            with feats[:-1], dim=-1)  # (time-1, batch)\\n    delta_min = deltas.min(dim=0, keepdim=True).values\\n    delta_max = deltas.max(dim=0, keepdim=True).values\\n    delta_norm = (deltas - delta_min) / (delta_max - delta_min + 1e-8)\\n    preferred_dt = 1.0 + (eff - 1.0) * (1.0 - delta_norm)\\n    dt_indices = torch.arange(1, max_dt + 1, dtype=torch.float32, device=feats.device)\\n    sigma = 1.0\\n    log_probs = -0.5 * ((preferred_dt.unsqueeze(-1) - Let me wait more. With `debug` config (pretrain=1, batch_size=10, batch_length=20), but `eval_episode_num` isn't overridden in debug config (defaults to 10). That means 10 eval episodes need to complete before training starts. Each episode = ~500 env steps. With 4 envs running in parallel and action_repeat=2, that's about 10/4 = 3 rounds of 500/action_repeat = 250 steps each.


Let me check if it's finished now.
```

</details>

The process is still running at 1328% CPU but the log is stuck at "Start evaluation." This is normal - DreamerV3 evaluation runs 10 episodes silently and only prints when done. With the `debug` config, it uses `batch_size: 10, batch_length: 20` for training but the eval is still running full episodes.

Let me wait a bit more. This should complete in a few minutes. Actually, with `--steps 100` and `debug` config (which has `prefill: 1`), the initial evaluation happens before any training. With 10 eval episodes and each being ~500 env steps, this takes a while. 

Since the agent starts with a random policy, each episode runs the full time limit of 1000 env steps divided by action_repeat, so I'll just let this continue.

But wait - looking at the CPU time, it's at 1328% (using many cores for the env). But the log is just buffered - even `-u` only unbuffers Python's own stdout, not the internal print calls from within the simulate function.

Let me kill this process and try with `--eval_episode_num 0` to skip evaluation entirely. 

That way I can focus on just testing the training loop without the evaluation overhead slowing things down.

## 汇报

### 改动文件

**configs.yaml** (1 行)
- L114: 新增 `gate_fixed_dt: 0`

**models.py** (8 处, ~18 行净增)
- L188-196: `WorldModel._train` — `gate_fixed_dt > 0` 时跳过 gate curriculum，直接用固定 `effective_max_dt` 和 `gate_scale=0.0`
- L245: gate loss 条件追加 `and self._config.gate_fixed_dt == 0`，跳过 gate loss 计算
- L314: gate metrics 条件追加 `and self._config.gate_fixed_dt == 0`，跳过 gate_loss/gate_scale/entropy_bonus 日志
- L547: `_imagine` 中 `gate_active` 条件追加 `and config.gate_fixed_dt == 0`
- L562: fallback dt 改用 `torch.full((batch,), fixed, ...)` 支持任意固定值
- L572-573: `init_dt` 乘以 `gate_fixed_dt`，确保第一步也用固定 dt

### dry-run 结果
- 训练正常启动，无报错
- `gate_dt_mean=2.0`, `gate_dt_std=0.0`, `gate_dt_2_frac=1.0` — dt 恒定为 2
- gate_loss、gate_scale、gate_entropy_bonus 未输出（符合预期）
- exp_005 不受影响（仍在运行）

### exp_006_fixed_dt2 启动命令

```bash
cd /root/autodl-tmp/event-gated-adaptive-horizon && \
source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base && \
export LD_PRELOAD=/usr/lib/x86_64-linux-gnu/libstdc++.so.6 && \
export MUJOCO_GL=osmesa && \
export PYOPENGL_PLATFORM=osmesa && \
CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=0 python dreamer.py \
  --configs defaults dmc_proprio \
  --task dmc_walker_walk \
  --logdir logdir/fixed_dt2_v1_s1 \
  --steps 150000 \
  --seed 1 \
  --compile False \
  --device cuda:0 \
  --gate_enabled True \
  --gate_fixed_dt 2
```

### 预估训练时长
exp_005 的 fps ~6.69 步/秒。Fixed dt 省去 gate forward pass（~200K 参数），预估 fps ~7-8。150K 步约 **5-6 小时**。

- L572-573: `init_dt` 乘以 `gate_fixed_dt`，确保第一步也用固定 dt

### dry-run 结果
- 训练正常启动，无报错
- `gate_dt_mean=2.0`, `gate_dt_std=0.0`, `gate_dt_2_frac=1.0` — dt 恒定为 2
- gate_loss、gate_scale、gate_entropy_bonus 未输出（符合预期）
- exp_005 不受影响（仍在运行）

### exp_006_fixed_dt2 启动命令

```bash
cd /root/autodl-tmp/event-gated-adaptive-horizon && \
source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base && \
export LD_PRELOAD=/usr/lib/x86_64-linux-gnu/libstdc++.so.6 && \
export MUJOCO_GL=osmesa && \
export PYOPENGL_PLATFORM=osmesa && \
CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=0 python dreamer.py \
  --configs defaults dmc_proprio \
  --task dmc_walker_walk \
  --logdir logdir/fixed_dt2_v1_s1 \
  --steps 150000 \
  --seed 1 \
  --compile False \
  --device cuda:0 \
  --gate_enabled True \
  --gate_fixed_dt 2
```

### 预估训练时长
exp_005 的 fps ~6.69 步/秒。Fixed dt 省去 gate forward pass（~200K 参数），预估 fps ~7-8。150K 步约 **5-6 小时**。
